性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和
証券グループ本社を親会社とする大和証券グループの会社です。内容に関する一切の権利は㈱大和総研にあります。無断での複製・転載・転送等はご遠慮ください。

2026年 11月 18日 全8頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 10月貿易統計 
自動車やAI関連の輸出が堅調も、原油高で貿易赤字は継続

経済調査部 エコノミスト ビリング 安奈

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 10月の貿易統計によると、輸出金額は前年比＋20.5％と 14カ月連続で増加し、
季節調整値でも前月比＋3.3％と 2カ月ぶりに増加した。輸入金額は前年比＋21.6％と
9カ月連続で増加した（季節調整値は前月比＋3.5％）。貿易収支は▲4,765億円、季節
調整値では▲7,672億円と、ともに 6カ月連続の赤字となった。 
◼ 中東情勢を受けた供給制約は続いているものの、代替調達の進展により 10月の原油及
び粗油（以下、原油）の輸入数量は前年同月とおおむね同水準となった。だが、輸入単
価は高止まりしており、中東情勢に起因する供給制約は原油高を通じて輸入金額を押
し上げ、貿易赤字が継続している。 
◼ 輸出数量（季節調整値）は前月比＋4.0％と 3カ月ぶりに増加した。乗用車や半導体等
製造装置、非鉄金属などの増加が全体を押し上げた。地域別では、米国向け、EU向け、
アジア向けのいずれも増加した。アジア向けのうち中国向けも増加した。 
◼ 先行きの輸出数量は、横ばい圏で推移するとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き下
支えしよう。ただし、リスク要因はAI関連投資の先行きと中東情勢だ。AI関連投資の
持続性には不透明感があり、資本財輸出の下押し要因となり得る。中東からの原油輸
出には回復の動きが見込まれているものの、中東情勢をめぐる不透明感は依然として
残る。

### Page 2

1

2 / 8

【貿易金額】自動車やAI関連の輸出が堅調も、原油高で貿易赤字は継続 
2026年10月の貿易統計によると、輸出金額は前年比＋20.5％とコンセンサス（同＋20.4％、
Bloomberg調査）とほぼ一致し、14カ月連続で増加した（図表 1）。輸出増加の主因は輸出価格
（同＋13.9％）の上昇だ。円安効果があったほか、旺盛な AI・半導体需要を背景に半導体等電
子部品や半導体等製造装置などの価格が上昇していることが寄与したとみられる。輸出の実勢
を示す輸出数量は同＋5.8％と堅調に推移した。世界的な AI需要を背景に半導体関連財の輸出
が堅調だったほか、9月に自動車の挽回生産が進んだこともあり、10月の自動車輸出は比較的
強めの動きになった可能性がある。また、燃料高を背景に燃費性能に優れているハイブリッド
車への需要の高まりも輸出増を後押ししたと考えられる。輸出金額の季節調整値は前月比＋
3.3％と 2カ月ぶりに増加した（図表 2左）。輸出価格（以下、数量と価格の季節調整値は大和
総研による）は低下した一方、輸出数量が上昇し、輸出金額を押し上げた。 
輸入金額は前年比＋21.6％と 9カ月連続で増加した（図表 1）。輸入の実勢を示す輸入数量は
同▲0.2％だった一方、輸入価格が同＋21.9％と大幅に上昇し、金額を押し上げた。輸入価格
の上昇の背景には、中東情勢による原油及び粗油（以下、原油）や液化石油ガスの価格上昇が
ある。特に、代替調達先となっている米国からの原油輸入は数量面（同＋738.2％）と価格面
（同＋384.6％）で上昇傾向が続いており、米国からの輸入金額は同＋1122.8％と高い伸び率と
なった。輸入金額の季節調整値は前月比＋3.5％と増加した（図表2右）。  
10 月の原油の輸入数量は前年比▲0.7％とおおむね前年並みとなったが、輸入価格（同＋
61.2％）が大きく上昇したことで輸入金額（同＋60.5％）が押し上げられた。こうした中東情
勢に起因する供給制約による原油価格の高騰が引き続き輸入金額を押し上げ、貿易赤字が継続
する要因となっている（図表 3）。貿易収支は▲4,765億円、季節調整値では▲7,672億円と、
ともに6カ月連続の赤字となった（図表 4）。

【輸出数量】主要国・地域向けがそろって増加 
輸出数量（季節調整値）は前月比＋4.0％と3カ月ぶりに増加した。乗用車の増加が全体を押
し上げたほか、グローバルなAI関連需要を背景に半導体等製造装置や非鉄金属も増加に寄与し
た。振れの大きい船舶も下支えした。地域別では、米国向け（同＋7.9％）、EU 向け（同＋
15.7％）、アジア向け（同＋0.5％）のいずれも増加した。アジア向けのうち中国向け（同＋
5.4％）も増加した（図表 5左）。  
米国向けは 3カ月ぶりに増加した。前月に自動車の挽回生産が進んだことや、燃料高により
ハイブリッド車の販売が好調であることから、乗用車は増加した。医薬品は前月の反動で増加
したほか、半導体等製造装置は AI需要の恩恵を受け、4カ月連続で増加した。 
EU向けは4カ月ぶりに増加した。対米輸出と同様に、乗用車の増加が大きく寄与した。EU向
けの乗用車輸出は 3カ月連続で堅調であり、ハイブリッド車への需要の高まりが反映されてい
る。二輪自動車や非鉄金属、原動機なども増加した。 
アジア向けと、そのうち中国向けはともに 2カ月連続で増加した。アジア向けの増加の主因
は、2カ月連続で振れの大きい船舶の増加だった。半導体等製造装置や乗用車、非鉄金属など
も増加した。中国向けも半導体等製造装置や乗用車、非鉄金属などが増加した。

【見通し】輸出数量は横ばい圏で推移の見込み、AI関連需要と中東情勢がリスク要因 
先行きの輸出数量は、横ばい圏で推移するとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き下支え要
因になろう。ただし、AI関連投資の先行きには不確実性もあり、その持続性には引き続き注意
が必要だ。また、中東からの原油輸出には回復の動きが見込まれているものの、中東情勢をめ
ぐる不透明感は依然として残る。 
11 月に入ってもホルムズ海峡周辺で船舶への攻撃が続いているが1、国際エネルギー機関
（IEA）の11月石油市場リポートによると、米国とイランの軍事衝突後初めて、原油市場は供給
超過に転じる見込みである。中東からの原油輸出が回復しつつあることは好材料であるものの、
同リポートでは下振れリスクが引き続き大きいとも指摘されている2。中東情勢の緊迫化に伴う
資源価格や海上輸送コスト（運賃・保険料）の高止まりが長期化すれば、物流制約に加え、海
外経済の下振れを通じて、輸出数量の下押し要因となり得る。 
米国向けは、AI関連需要を背景とした米国の設備投資の増加が追い風となり、増加すると見
込まれる。ただし、一部のAI関連企業では収益性や財務面への懸念が広がっているほか、米中
間選挙で民主党が上下両院を奪還することが確実となったことで、AIやデータセンターをめぐ
る規制強化への懸念も高まっている。設備投資の拡大が持続するか不透明だ3。設備投資の増勢
が鈍化すれば、日本からの資本財輸出にも下押し圧力がかかる可能性がある。 
アジア向けは横ばい圏で推移するだろう。このうち中国向けはやや軟調な推移が見込まれる。
中国の 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5％となり、2026年の政府成長率目標で
ある 4.5％～5.0％の下限と同水準となった4。中国では内需が引き続き低迷している。景気刺
激策による下支え効果が期待されるものの、需要の弱さが日本からの輸出の逆風になる構図は
続く見込みだ。 
欧州向けは横ばい圏での推移となろう。欧州では、ドイツを中心とした財政拡張を背景に、
防衛支出やインフラ投資が景気を下支えすると見込まれる5。これが欧州経済の回復につながれ
ば、日本からの財輸出も持ち直すことが期待される。ただし、足元では長期金利の上昇が加速
しており、これにより設備投資や公共投資の拡大が抑制される恐れがある。また、エネルギー
価格の高止まりによる個人消費の下振れも重しとなり得る。

1 「中東でタンカー攻撃続く、緊張緩和期待に逆風」（ロイター、2026年11月 10日公開、同 11月 11日更
新） 
2 International Energy Agency (IEA) “Oil Market Report - November 2026”（2026年11月13日） 
3 畑中宏仁・神田慶司「ハイパースケーラーの投資動向が世界経済に与える影響」（大和総研レポート、2026
年 11月 12日） 
4 齋藤尚登「中国経済見通し：不動産不況の底打ちに期待」（大和総研レポート、2026年10月22日） 
5 橋本政彦「7-9月期ユーロ圏GDP 減速しつつも上振れ」（大和総研レポート、2026年11月2日）

2026 年 10 月 30 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）予測

～ほぼゼロ成長も、緩やかな景気回復は継続～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田 隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表する 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.0%（前期比年
率換算＋0.1%）と予測する。設備投資や政府消費の増加に支えられ、かろうじて 4 四半期連続のプラス成長を
維持したものの、個人消費や輸出が前期の一時的な押し上げ要因の剥落もあって減少したことで、ほぼゼロ成長
にとどまったと見込まれる。なお、輸入が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は大きめのマイナスと
なるものの、公的在庫のプラス寄与により、おおむね相殺されたと考えられる。 
また、名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.9%（年率換算＋3.8%）となったと見込まれる。コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進
む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比＋2.7%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と高めの伸びが続いた。 
今回、7～9 月期の実質 GDP はほぼゼロ成長にとどまる見込みだが、イラン情勢の悪化という逆風を踏まえれ
ば、国内景気は底堅さを維持している。先行きについては、輸入コストの高止まりに伴う価格転嫁の進展が景気
の重しとなる可能性はあるものの、良好な雇用・所得環境や企業業績の改善を背景に内需の回復基調が維持さ
れることなどから、国内景気は緩やかな回復を続けると期待される。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度 
（出所）内閣府「四半期別 GDP 速報」により作成

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－0.1%） 
 良好な所得環境が維持される中、消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かったものの、前期に耐久財
が自動車やエアコンを中心に一時的な要因によって増加した反動が生じたとみられる。価格転
嫁の進展による物価上昇、地震や台風といった自然災害の発生も重しとなった可能性がある。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－0.3%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数が横ばいとなる中、建築コストが上昇していることもあり、住
宅投資は小幅ながら減少が続いたとみられる。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比＋0.9%） 
 企業の投資意欲は省力化やデジタル需要などに支えられて堅調であり、設備投資は 3 四半期
ぶりに増加したと見込まれる。機械設備投資の供給側統計である資本財総供給（除く輸送機械）
も 7～9 月期は前期比＋5.3%と増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度－0.0%ポイント） 
 前期に供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫などの積み増しを進めた反動もあ
り、7～9 月期はマイナス寄与になったと考えられる。 
 なお、公的在庫については、政府による代替調達の進展により石油備蓄の減少ペースが大きく鈍
化したことから、前期比寄与度＋0.5%ポイントとプラス寄与になったとみられる。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費支出（前期比＋0.3%） 
 高齢化が進む中、医療費・介護費を中心に底堅く推移したと予測する。 
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－2.7%） 
 建築・土木コストの上昇が続いている中、7～8 月の建設総合統計（出来高ベース：公共）は弱
めの動きとなったことから、実質公共投資は 2 四半期連続で減少したと見込まれる。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度－0.5%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比－0.5%、実質輸入：同＋1.7%） 
 輸出は前期比－0.5%と減少したと予測する。財は堅調な米国向けや中東向けの持ち直しを受
けて増加した一方、サービスは前期に研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れた反動
で減少したとみられる。 
 輸入は前期比＋1.7%と増加したと予測する。特に財が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直した。ただ
し、サービスについては減少した可能性がある。 
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は－0.5%ポイントとマイナスになったと見込ま
れる。

本刊載之報告為台新投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效
性，審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬台新投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

聯強(2347)：資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能，但長期營益率改善趨勢
不變 
建議：維持買進

一、聯強為亞太地區最大 3C 通
路商，營收成長以半導體業務最
佳  
 聯強為 3C 電子產品之銷售、配送、維修、量身訂做即時生產之專業通路商，為
亞太地區 3C 通路商龍頭，2026 年前三季產品比重分別為商用加值佔 31%、資
訊消費佔 17% 、 半 導 體 佔 48% 、 手 機 產 品 佔 4% ，成長主要來商用加值
(+112%YoY)和半導體業務(+162%YoY)。聯強海外市場包含中國、香港、紐澳、
泰國、印尼、印度及越南等地，其中以中國、台灣和印尼等區域較佳，營收分別
為 YoY+109%、YoY+78%和 YoY+18%。聯強競爭優勢為透過其四合一經營模
式，搭配其綿密的通路發揮規模經濟，並提供高附加價值的後勤運籌(庫存管理
與快速維為修服務等)，於中國主要競爭對手為神州數碼、天音通信控股。

二、半導體業務成長性佳，3Q26 
EPS 2.93 元創新高並優於預期 
 3Q26 營收 2366 億元(+18%QoQ；+136%YoY)優於預估值 14%，其中商用加
值和半導體營收分別為+116%YOY 和+273%YoY，因秉棄部分利潤不佳專案，
毛利率 3.49%優於前季之 3.4%，營業利益 61.41 億元(+28%%QoQ)優於預估
值 24%，因營運資金需求以及籌資成本上升致使利息費用提高至 9.34 億元
(+106%QoQ)，稅後純益 48.83 億元(+25%QoQ；+90%YoY)，EPS 2.93 元優
於預期之 2.5 元並創新高。

三、資金成本上揚導致業務拓展
較為謹慎，估計 4Q26 EPS 2.34
元 
 由於資金成本上揚致使公司放棄部分利潤不佳半導體業務，十月營收月減 34%，
估計 4Q26 營收 2046 億元(-14%QoQ；YoY+62%)，因產品組合變化轉佳，毛
利率提升至 3.58%，稅後純益 38.99 億元(-20%QoQ；+41%YoY)，EPS 2.34
元。

四、AI 長線需求有助商用加值和
半導體業務成長性 
 集邦 預估 2026 和 2027 年全 球九 大 CSP 資 本支 出分 別為 0.95 兆 美元
(+106%YoY)和 1.53 兆美元(+60%YoY)，且 AI 需求從雲端擴展到企業端，其中
以商用加值和半導體業務受惠幅度最大。 
在商用加值業務方面，在台灣和中國資料中心產品出貨挹注下，3Q26 營收
+116%YoY。展望 2027 年，中國十十五規劃和國產化政策推動政府和國企加速
採用本土品牌，推升聯強代理華為、浪潮等品牌業務成長性，台灣則是接獲數個
半導體大廠 AI 基礎建設專案，加計中小企業 AI 建置需求逐步顯現，商用業務將
為營運成長主要動能之一。 
在半導體業務方面，缺貨和漲價效致使記憶體、SSD 和硬碟業務為半導體業務
中 成 長 性 最 佳 者 ， 且 AI 推 論 亦 增 加 CPU 需求，3Q26 半 導 體 業 務 營 收

YoY+273%，半導體業務為營運成長另一重點業務，但因資金成本上揚，公司將
摒棄部分利潤未達目標專案，此將影響短期營收動能。 
受惠於出貨 ASP 增加，以及原廠擴大促銷力道挽救銷售量，3Q26 資訊家用和
手機業務分別+21%YoY 和+33%YoY。記憶體大漲致使原廠逐步反應成本於 PC
和手機新機售價，並將導致消費者延長換機週期，2027 年此兩塊業務成長性仍
相對保守看待。

五、資金成本上揚將致使接案更
為謹慎，但長期營益率好轉方向
不變 
 聯強今年接獲數個大型 AI 基礎建設專案，致使營收動能以及營運資金需求大增，
3Q26 遇到資金成本上揚，致使利息費用較前季倍增，公司將透過聯貸、可轉債
以及現增降低利息負擔，預期三項籌資工具皆完成後可望降低利息費用 20-
30%。展望 2027 年，儘管營收成長性不如 2026 年亮眼，但接單策略轉趨謹慎
將致使毛利率下滑壓力降低，且預期營業費用增幅有限下，營益率持續好轉方向
不變。

六、維持買進理由  1)預估 2026 和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 9.38 元和 11.07 元。 
2)儘管資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能，但長期營益率改善趨勢不變，維持買進
評等，TP 100 元 (2027 PE x 9)。

Silergy Corp.  | Asia Pacific
  3Q26 Margin miss from higher 
production cost and opex; stay 
UW 
3Q26 results miss from incremental production cost and higher opex: Silergy’s 
3Q26 GM of 47.4% and OPM of 13.3% missed MSe and consensus despite a better 
product mix (faster growth in AI and automotive). We attribute the miss to rising 
foundry/OSAT costs and higher AI datacenter R&D. Management guided opex to 
stay roughly flat in 1H27 and that it could be higher in 2H27 with new hires.
AI-related revenue and Gen4 process are key drivers for 2027 margin recovery: 
Management said 2027 revenue could grow at least 20-30% YoY, with AI-related 
revenue at c.25% and Gen4 at 50% of total revenue by end-2027. Management also 
guided 2027 GM to be >50% while continuing passing on cost to customers.
Automotive revenue could account for 20% of 2027 total revenue: Management 
expects strong automotive growth in 2027, with dollar content per high-end vehicle 
rising from US$220 in 2026 to US$270-300 in 2027.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Silergy Corp. (6415.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 9.5% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6%
risk premium, beta of 1.25), an intermediate growth rate of 13.5%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Gross margin expansion amid stabilized competition
n A surge in LED demand as pricing drops to the sweet spot
n Greater-than-expected benefits from China's support in local semiconductors

n Higher-than-expected dividend payout ratio
Risks to Downside
n Lower gross margin amid intensified competition
n Weaker-than-expected benefits from China's support in local semiconductors
n Lower-than-expected dividend payout ratio

12-18 months.

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Egis Technology Inc

Shenzhen Goodix Technology
Co Ltd (603160.SS) From To
Price Target Rmb58.00 Rmb40.00
Higher memory costs are squeezing handset makers’ budgets for
displays, touch controllers and sensors. We expect weak
Android volumes to limit DDIC suppliers’ ability to pass on
higher wafer and packaging costs, even where new OLED
programs lift content per phone.
Android bears most of the decline. Our US hardware team (2026 AlphaWise

Smartphone Survey: Another Down Year That's Not Yet Priced In) expects 2027
smartphone shipments to fall 5%, versus 4% growth previously ( Exhibit 2 ). The
team's bear case is a global decline of 11%. The base case has Android down about
9% to 768mn units and iPhone up 7% to 270mn. Across the US, China, the UK and
Germany, 41% of owners were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months, down
2ppt ( Exhibit 3 ). The survey implies a replacement cycle of 4.4 years in 2027, up

from 4.2. We expect Android supply chains to face the greatest pressure in 2027:
falling shipments, continued de-specing, and margin compression leave Android
OEMs and suppliers more exposed as brands attempt to offset higher component
costs through a combination of price increases and bill of materials (BOM)
reductions.
China's replacement demand is weakening. We expect a 7% decline in China
smartphone shipments in 2027, following an estimated 12% decline in 2026. Among

China survey respondents, 48% were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months,
down from 49% last year. When asked why they chose their current phone, 17%
cited built-in AI, down from 22%, while 35% cited overall device quality, up from
32% ( Exhibit 4 ).
Brand switching favors Apple in China ( Exhibit 6 ). Among respondents in China,
Apple had the strongest net switching intention at +28% of its installed base. The
corresponding rates for Xiaomi, Oppo, Vivo and Honor ranged from -21% to -31%. A

shift in purchases toward Apple would put further further pressure on component
demand at these four Android brands.
Stock implications: Egis: Rating downgraded to UW and price target lowered from
NT$105 to NT$66; we estimate wider losses in 2026, lower our 2027 EPS estimate
25% and introduce 2028 estimates. Himax: Remain EW, price target down from US
$17.4 to US$9.2, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down 17%/17%/33%. Goodix: Remain
UW, PT down from Rmb58 to Rmb40, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down

communications with a subject company, public appearances

Himax: Earnings Revision and Quarterly Financials
We lower 2026/27/28 EPS estimates 17%/17%/33%: We incorporate 2Q-3Q26 actual
results. We see continued weakness in the consumer market (especially smartphones) due
to elevated component costs and weak demand. Increasing material and foundry prices
might also affect Himax's margins, as we believe it is unlikely to pass through to clients.
However, we expect 2027 to see more contributions from OLED DDIC, OLED T-con

shipments and WiseEye opportunities, which should help revenue and gross margin.
Continued OLED shipments for a mainstream model and inventory builds for new
premium phones lifted combined LCD and OLED handset IC sales in 3Q26. Management
expects smartphone driver sales to decline Q/Q in 4Q26. Its LCD touch-and-display drivers
serve the entry and mid-range segments, in which pressure to cut component costs is
greatest.

Himax: Valuation Methodology
We remain EW and lower our price target to US$9.2: This factors in our lower
expectations for 2026-28 earnings. Our beta assumption is raised from 1.3 to 1.5 (leading
to an increased cost of equity from 9.9% to 11.0%); the increase in beta reflects the higher
uncertainties around end market demand and component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure.
Our terminal growth rate assumption is cut from 3.4% to 2.0%; the new terminal growth

rate is now more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed
economies over the longer term. Other residual income assumptions are unchanged,
including risk-free rate of 2%, risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 8.0%.  We
lower our bull/bear-case values from US$26.0/8.7 to US$13.7/4.6.

10
Goodix: Estimate Revisions
We  lower our 2026/27/28 earnings estimates 14%/18%/5%. We factor in 2-3Q26 actual
results, and reduce our earnings forecasts to reflect weak smartphone end market
demand and cost pressures.  Smartphones accounted for 62.6% of 1H26 sales, and weaker
demand and chip pricing weighed on 3Q revenue. Xiaomi’s 18 Pro series uses two Goodix
touch controllers and its ultrasonic fingerprint sensor. Wider adoption of integrated OLED

touch and display drivers (TDDI) would displace standalone touch controllers. Goodix’s
own OLED TDDI was still in internal testing in October.

Goodix: Valuation Methodology
We maintain our UW rating. We lower our price target to Rmb40 from Rmb58: This is
based on a residual income model using our base-case forecasts. The lower price target
reflects our lower earnings estimate. We also lower our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.9% to 2.5%. The new terminal growth rate is now more in line with the macro
economic growth rate of global economies over the longer term.

Other key RI assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 8.4%, an
intermediate growth rate of 16.5%. We lower our bull-case value to Rmb55 from Rmb80
and our bear-case value to Rmb21 from Rmb31.

Egis: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We widen our 2026 loss estimate and lower our 2027 EPS estimate 25%; we introduce
our 2028 earnings estimate: We incorporate 1-3Q26 actual results and roll forward our
earnings estimates to 2028. We continue to believe Egis will remain loss-making in 2026,
given its ongoing investment in new businesses and limited new project wins for Alcor in
the near term.  We lower our 2027 estimates due to smartphone market weakness.

Egis: Valuation methodology
We downgrade our rating to UW and lower our price target from NT$105 to NT$66:
This is our base-case scenario value derived from a residual income model. This reflects
the changes in our 2026-27 earnings estimates. We roll forward our valuation date from
2025 to 2026.
Changes in our RI assumptions include:
• Cost of equity is raised to 11.0% from 9.5% (beta raised from 1.25 to 1.50). The
increase in beta reflects the higher uncertainties around end market demand and

component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure
• Intermediate growth rate cut from 14.8% to 8.0%. The decrease in intermediate
growth rate reflects our more moderate view on the end market demand recovery
and new products ramp up schedule
• Terminal growth rate cut from 5.0% to 2.0%. The new terminal growth rate is now
more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed economies
over the longer term
Other key RI assumptions remain unchanged:

• Risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%
• Payout ratio of 40%
We lower our bull-case value from  NT$143 to NT$95 and bear-case value from NT$49 to
NT$30, implying 24x and 7x 2027 EPS, respectively, on our estimates.

STOCK RATINGS

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

穩懋(3105.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
2027 光通訊跟衛星本業雙引擎 認列聯茂收益業外動能強勁 
 投資評價與建議 
公司過往Forward PER落在20~30倍，考量光通訊與衛星業務出貨放量，毛利
率迎來結構性跳升，有利估值重估，維持「增加持股」評等。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收符合預期，毛利率因產品組合優化上升至37.4%：3Q26營收58.6億
元，季增11%，營收符合預期主要係因傳統消費性電子旺季，加上航太衛星與
光電產品動能強勁，毛利率37.4%，季增9.2個百分點，主要係因產品組合優化，
航太衛星與光電產品營收占比過半，加上產能利用率上升至75%，業外收入
6.98億元，主因聯茂貢獻業外EPS約1.61元，稅後EPS 4.55元。 
4Q26營運淡季不淡，2027成⻑動能聚焦Optical跟Infrastructure：(1)4Q26
面臨手機傳統淡季與年底例行性歲修，但受惠於PD產能持續開出與其他產品
線營收持平至微幅成⻑，預估4Q26營收將季增1~3%，毛利率37~39%。(2)光
通訊PD於3Q26逐步放量，4Q26將持續成⻑，預估2027年大幅放量，CW Laser
正進行可靠度測試，預估2027年小量貢獻，顯著營收爆發期落在2028年，EML
處於產品開發與測試階段，2028年才有機會產生營收貢獻。(3)航太衛星帶動
Infrastructure成為3Q26季增幅度最高的產品線，衛星佔Infrastructure營收
比重已升至40%，超越傳統基地台。(4)預估聯茂2027年EPS 72.22元，穩懋採
權益法認列持股23%之投資收益約61.7億元，貢獻EPS約14.56元。 
 投資風險 
光通PD跟雷射放量不如預期、衛星發射遞延。

| 營運概況 
3Q26 產品組合優化帶動毛利率與 EPS 創高：3Q26 營收 58.6 億元，季增 11%，營收符合預期主要係因傳統消費
性電子旺季，加上航太衛星與光電產品動能強勁，毛利率 37.4%，季增 9.2 個百分點，優於財測，主要係因產品
組合優化，航太衛星與光電產品營收占比過半，加上產能利用率上升至 75%，營業利益率 24.2%，業外收入 6.98
億元，因持有聯茂 23%股權並採權益法認列投資收益，聯茂貢獻業外 EPS 約 1.61 元，稅後 EPS 4.55 元。 
4Q26 營運淡季不淡，2027 成⻑動能聚焦 Optical 跟 Infrastructure：(1)儘管 4Q26 面臨手機傳統淡季與年底例
行性歲修，但受惠於 PD 產能持續開出與其他產品線營收持平至微幅成⻑，預估 4Q26 營收將季增 1~3%，毛利
率 37~39%，主要受惠產品組合優化跟產能利用率維持 70%以上。(2)中階手機受臨中國 PA 供應鏈本土化及記憶
體缺貨的逆風，但穩懋聚焦中高階手機市場，動能維持穩健，4Q26 Cellular 營收僅呈現小幅下滑，WiFi 產品在
Router 需求回流下，3Q26 雙位數成⻑，4Q26 呈現持平至微幅成⻑。(3)光通訊 PD 於 3Q26 逐步放量，4Q26
將持續成⻑，預估 2027 年產能完全開出並大幅放量，CW Laser 正進行可靠度測試，預估 2027 年小量貢獻，顯
著營收爆發期落在 2028，EML 處於產品開發與測試階段，2028 年才有機會產生營收貢獻。(4)航太衛星帶動
Infrastructure 成為 3Q26 季增幅度最高的產品線，衛星佔 Infrastructure 營收比重已升至 40%，超越傳統基地
台，目前 GaN 月產能是 300 片左右，公司積極擴充 GaN 產能，預計 1Q27 導入 GaN 6 吋製程生產。(5)聯茂
2027 年爆發性成⻑，預估聯茂 2027 年 EPS 72.22 元，穩懋採權益法認列持股 23%之投資收益約 61.7 億元，貢
獻EPS約14.56元。

| 公 司 簡 介 
穩懋(3105)為全球最大的化合物半導體晶圓代工廠，核心業務聚焦於微波通訊與光電元件的砷化鎵(GaAs)及氮化
鎵(GaN)晶圓代工服務，公司總部及研發中心設於台灣桃園龜山(華亞科技園區)，主要六吋晶圓生產基地位於桃
園華亞廠及龜山廠，總產能約 4.3 萬片，3Q26 產能利用率為 75%。3Q26 年營收比重為 Cellular(手機
PA)25~30%、Infrastructure(通訊基礎建設)35~40%、Wi-Fi 15~20%、Optical 10~15%、其他 3%。主要客
戶包含 Broadcom、Lumentum、Murata 等國際大廠，銷售地區為台灣 8%、亞洲 70%、美洲 15%、歐洲
7%。 
穩懋的微波通訊產品處理高頻訊號的放大與切換，主要元件為異質接面雙載子電晶體(HBT)與假晶高電子遷移率
電晶體(pHEMT) ，用於智慧型手機功率放大器(PA)、Wi-Fi 路由器及低軌衛星設備的射頻(RF)訊號，提供低雜

營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持上品（4770）買進之投資建議，主要考量：（1）半導體相
關產業進入百年一遇的擴產期，帶動相關廠商擴廠、擴線，以及對應
的運輸設備需求規劃大幅度提升，上品目前在手訂單由2026 年初的38
億提升至 80 億歷史新高，公司對於 2027 年營收展望由先前的+20%YoY
提升至+35-40%YoY。（2）2H25 至 3Q26 的 GM 反映前一年度景氣低迷
時的接單，隨著產業需求提升，訂單能見度已拉升至超過一年，公司
可擇優進行生產，未來一段時間公司 GM 將明顯改善，逐季往上提升至
單季 40%水準。（3）現有產能已達到滿載，2026 至 2028 年需求強勁
增長，以內襯設備為主的新廠將於 2026 年底開始貢獻營收，4Q26 至
1Q27 營運將出現顯著提升，再來將進入非常忙碌且全力拉升產能的時
期，2027 年彰濱廠產能可望+20%YoY，嘉興廠產能將年增 30%YoY，槽
車產能經擴充後，已由 2026 年初的約當 80 台提升至目前的 130-140
台，2027 年中更可望擴增至 180 台，公司對於未來展望看法非常樂觀。
研究部預估 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 14.4 元、22.48 元(原估 12.88 元、
17.34 元)，分別+41.2%YOY、+56.1%YOY。考量目前回到半導體加速擴
廠循環，有利於上品營運回溫，過往 PER 評價區間落在 10-24 倍，目
前評價僅 10.5 倍，風險報酬比極佳，故維持上品買進之投資建議，目
標價 340 元（15xPER *2027EPS）。

重點摘要： 
1. 上品為氟素樹脂(鐵氟龍)生產商

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

上品為各項氟素樹脂(又稱鐵氟龍PFA)的生產商，提供塗層、
酸洗槽、管路系統等全方面鐵氟龍產品，主要用於高溫或腐
蝕性化學液之生產、儲存、純化、輸送等功能之設備及材料。
產能為台灣彰濱廠及中國浙江嘉興廠。

2. 上品內襯設備產品占比最高，另外也包括氟素樹脂材料及工
程收入 
上品的產品主要為因應半導體及各產業所需的氟素樹脂(包
括PTFE、M-PTFE、PFA、PFA-HP、PFA HP PLUS等產品)。產品
線包括氟素樹脂內襯板材與素板，內襯直管用管材，客製化
射出件與焊接材料等。

半導體製程中，所有進來的原料都必須避開金屬離子，需大
量使用氟素材料的純化系統(可避免溫度交換、反應或吸收)。
氟素樹脂內襯產品廣泛應用於半導體設備、生化科技，製藥
及精密化學業等高純度要求的槽車、桶槽和所有藥液接觸的
容器，以及客製化的熱交換器、溢流槽與治具。

圖 2、上品 3Q26 產業別營收比重

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

此外，上品的氟素工程收入主要是因應半導體及各產業客戶
所需，提供流體力學計算，高純化學品設備系統之設計規劃，
製造及安裝等一套完整服務。上品的競爭優勢為鐵氟龍下游
應用的研發能力強，產品自製率高達80-90%，可依據不同的材
料特性進行調整，客製化開發下游客戶所需的新產品。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

在競爭格局上，上品為產業龍頭，且極具優勢，能跟公司競爭
的只有兩家日商，但這兩家日商的市佔率加總還不到公司的
一半。

3. 2026-2028年建廠需求顯著提升，在手訂單創歷史新高 
2H24開始，受到中國地區內捲競爭影響，拖累上品毛利率表現
從過往的45-50%區間下降至25-30%區間，中國地區營收占比
也逐漸下降。

圖 3、上品 3Q26 地區營收比重

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

目前來看，台灣地區占比持續擴大，且此趨勢將延續下去，主
因為主要客戶對中國供應鏈有所限制，公司未來主要將透過
台灣產能供應台灣、美國、日本、新加坡等中國以外市場。

毛利率表現取決於與客戶的接單狀況，2026前三季上品整體
毛利率已回升至35%, +0.8pctYoY。由於2025年市況不佳，所
以2H25至1H26出貨的訂單毛利率較低，然2H26年開始進入產
業復甦階段，目前市況已明顯比2025年樂觀。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

圖 4、上品在手訂單創歷史新高

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

2026年初上品的在手訂單為38億元，但截至最新統計，在手訂
單已飆升至新台幣80億元。主要客戶的需求極為急迫，甚至
在半導體展期間派遣團隊到展會攤位確保供貨。

目前上品最核心的重點就是急速擴充產能，公司對2026年至
2028年的市場需求非常樂觀，2027年現有產線預計將全數塞
滿，必須持續擴產才能滿足客戶需求。

4. 百年一遇的半導體業擴廠熱潮 
上品近幾年成長動能皆來自於半導體業，由庫存儲備、運輸
及循環等需求同步帶動。公司觀察2026年為百年一遇的半導
體業擴廠熱潮。如台積電在論壇中所說，目前全球同時有近
20個廠在建，包括台灣本土13座，海外5-6座。在如此大規模
建廠下，半導體化學品的供應量完全不足，因而啟動了整個
供應鏈的建廠、擴產與運輸需求。尤其是先進封裝需要多種
類、小劑量的功能性化學品，自2025年起需求就非常快速成
長。預期2026年至2028年都將維持極強勁的需求。

上品對未來成長非常樂觀，目前的挑戰在於產能如何滿足客
戶的急迫訂單。這需要時間逐步擴增產能、培養技術人員及
拓展生產空間，同時也必須協調金屬加工下包供應鏈跟上步
伐，接下來將進入全力拉升產能的時期。

5. 預估4Q26稅後EPS 4.5元，+5.5%QoQ，+150.3%YoY 
上品2026 前三季營收39.6 億元，+7.8%YoY ，GM 35% ，
+0.8pctYoY，前三季累計GM轉為年增，而3Q26 GM呈現季減主
因為配合客戶出貨排程，3Q26認列了2025年景氣低谷期所承
接之較低毛利訂單，前三季累計營業利益9.1億元，+6.9%YoY，
稅後EPS 9.9元，+17.8%YoY。

10月營收4.9億元，-15.5%MoM，+79.3%YoY，營收達成率29.1%，
符合過往10月營收佔比平均約31%之預期，研究部預估上品
4Q26營收可望達16.8億元，+5.1%QoQ，+59.8%YoY，隨著營運
逐漸好轉，GM可望季增至36.9%，+2.3pctQoQ，+8.3pctYoY，
稅後EPS 4.5元，+5.5%QoQ，+150.3%YoY

6. 預估2026、2027年稅後EPS 14.4元、22.48元，分別+41.2%YoY、
+56.1%YoY 
展望2026年，全球半導體加速擴廠，上品目前在手訂單由2026
年初的38億迅速提升到80億歷史新高。研究部預估2026年營收
56.4億元(原估51.6億元)，+19.4%YoY，隨著營運逐漸好轉，GM
可望擴增至35.6%，+2.6pctYoY，稅後EPS 14.4元(原估12.88
元)，+41.2%YoY。

現有產線於預計將於2027年將全數塞滿，隨著新廠產能開出，
預計2027年整體營收將提升35-40%(原為年增20%)。研究部預估
2027年營收76.8億元(原估62.7億元)，+36.2%YoY，隨著營運逐
漸好轉，GM可望略增至39.5%，+3.9pctYoY，稅後EPS 22.48元
(原估17.34元)，+56.1%YoY。

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(21배, 
15배, 12배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(LTA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 LTA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 LTA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 LTA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

### Page 4

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 29 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 09 월 30 일)

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q 부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520 억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1 조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV 에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

Document 250: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI 로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000 원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500 원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면 이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event
Establishment of the IRDAI (Insurance Regulatory
and Development Authority of India)
Acceptance of market entry applications from
domestic private firms and foreign entities
(foreign equity cap set at 26%)

through banks or bank-owned distribution
channels)

for the sale of new insurance products (replacing

3 
incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

### Page 6

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

5 
■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate.

Document 278: Asia Outlook 2027_ Growth broadens, capital gets selective

### Page 1

After a stronger-than-expected 2026, Asian growth should
broaden in 2027 as AI tailwinds moderate, lower energy
prices support demand and supply-chain diversiﬁcation
continues. Capital ﬂows will become more selective as global
ﬁnancial conditions tighten. We favor the KRW, TWD, SGD, and
expect Australia and Philippines to lead easing moves
Deepali Bhargava
How did Asia’s economy perform in 2026,
and what will look different in 2027?
2026 was stronger than expected

Asia surprised on the upside in 2026, with regional GDP growth
outperforming expectations, led by strong exports and investment
activity. Investment remained robust across much of the region,
supported by technology-related spending and government-led
infrastructure projects. This enabled export-oriented economies
such as South Korea, Taiwan and Singapore to signiﬁcantly exceed
consensus growth forecasts.

### Page 2

The picture was less favourable in economies more reliant on
domestic demand. Indonesia and the Philippines disappointed
expectations as household consumption remained subdued.
Higher energy prices weighed on real incomes, while the Philippines
also faced a drag from slower government spending amid political
and corruption-related controversies. Both economies attracted
relatively less tech-related foreign direct investment than the
region's manufacturing and semiconductor hubs, limiting support

from global dynamics.
India was a notable outperformer. Despite beneﬁting less directly
from the AI-driven boom and facing higher energy import costs, it
expanded by a robust 8.2% year-on-year in the ﬁrst half of 2026.
Growth continued to be supported by structural reforms, public
capital expenditure and subsidies that cushioned households from
higher fuel prices.
AI-led divergences should narrow in 2027, favouring South Asia
Overall, we expect GDP growth excluding China to slow to 3.8% in

2027, down from 4.5% in 2026. Tight chip supply should continue
to support investment growth through 2027, but as capex growth
slows from today's exceptional pace, AI's contribution to growth is
likely to moderate.
As AI tailwinds fade and the full eﬀects of monetary tightening are
felt, growth in developed Asia, particularly Korea and Australia,
could slow more than in developing Asia. Korea is the clearest
example, where growth has been concentrated in technology-

related sectors while domestic demand, non-tech industries and
broader wage growth have lagged. As in previous investment-led
booms, the beneﬁts have been slower to ﬁlter through to the wider
economy.
By contrast, the Philippines’ 2026 growth disappointment was
driven primarily by policy and political uncertainty, both of which
are likely to prove temporary. India continues to occupy a relative
sweet spot. While it beneﬁted less from the AI boom, it’s also less

exposed to any correction. Structural reforms, infrastructure

spending, and resilient services exports should leave it relatively
well placed as the AI cycle normalises.
The divergence is less pronounced in parts of Southeast Asia,
particularly Singapore and Malaysia, where domestic demand has
remained relatively resilient alongside a strong external sector.
Trade growth likely to moderate in 2027
Asian economies are expected to be the largest contributors to
global trade volume growth for the third consecutive year in 2026.

According to World Trade Organisation forecasts, global trade
growth is expected to moderate in 2027 as AI-related demand
cools from exceptionally strong levels, though the deceleration
should be gradual rather than abrupt.
Recent data has been mixed. Some signs of moderation appear in
semiconductor export growth in Japan and Taiwan, and export
orders softened across the board in October. However,
semiconductor exports from Korea and China continue to post

strong gains. Data from Taiwan suggest it’s still too early to call a
turning point.
Even so, trade growth should remain relatively resilient in 2027,
supported by ongoing supply-chain diversiﬁcation, continued
investment ﬂows and still-elevated levels of AI-related activity. This
is even as the pace of expansion normalises.
Pockets of domestic demand strength should support growth
The structural backdrop for consumption in Japan is becoming
more supportive. Wage growth has increased signiﬁcantly since the

start of the year. Also, households are gradually shifting away from
the cash-heavy behaviour associated with the deﬂation era
towards greater participation in ﬁnancial markets. This supports
wealth creation and consumption.
Singapore should continue to see resilient consumption growth,
supported by strong FDI inﬂows and spillovers from the AI
investment cycle. These tailwinds are feeding into ﬁrm wage
growth and stronger household purchasing power. Singapore has

How is AI reshaping Asia's growth story,
and can the region continue to beneﬁt as
the boom matures?
Looking ahead to 2027, North Asia should continue to dominate AI
trade. Southeast Asia should play an increasingly important role as
its AI story is driven by production, investment and supply-chain

integration rather than AI adoption itself. While the region doesn’t
have the largest AI companies, it’s become indispensable to
hardware and manufacturing ecosystems that make AI possible.
As ﬁrms diversify supply chains and invest in AI infrastructure,
Southeast Asia stands out as one of the largest structural
beneﬁciaries of the AI boom outside North Asia.

### Page 5

Trade dominance: Asia accounted for about 77% of AI-driven
exports in 2025
Asia sits at the centre of the global AI hardware ecosystem. It
dominates global trade in AI-enabling goods. East Asia, excluding
China, accounts for more than 40% of global exports of AI-related
products. Southeast Asia contributes another quarter despite
having a much smaller share of overall world trade. With China
contributing another 13%, Asia is generating much of the world's

growth in AI-related exports.
The same dynamic is visible on the import side. AI-enabling goods
account for a far larger share of imports than East Asia’s and
Southeast Asia’s share of overall trade, underscoring the deep
integration of global AI supply chains.
Growth in the ﬁrst half of 2026 reinforced Asia's leadership, with the
region's largest AI exporters recording the fastest growth. In the
second quarter, exports of AI-enabling goods surged by 80% YoY.

East Asia’s contribution to global export growth doubled to 32.3
percentage points in just six months, while Southeast Asia’s share
similarly surged to 22.0 percentage points. China alone accounted
for a further 12.8 percentage points, underscoring the region’s
outsized role in driving global trade.
These trade relationships have shifted markedly over the last
decade. One notable change is Southeast Asia's growing share of
US imports, consistent with the rapid build-out of data centres by

hyperscaler ﬁrms. Yet Southeast Asia has lagged in strengthening
trade linkages with East Asia, excluding China, and within the
region itself. This suggests there’s scope for deeper regional
integration as the AI ecosystem expands.
Broadening beneﬁciaries: Asia’s AI story extends beyond
Korea/Taiwan
On products, much of the focus has been on memory chip exports
from Korea and Taiwan. But the beneﬁciaries of the AI boom are
increasingly broadening. ASEAN economies have attracted

substantial foreign direct investment in semiconductors ucand

increased their share of global non-memory chip production.
Singapore and Malaysia now account for more than 33% of global
ampliﬁer chip exports, up from 23% a decade ago. The Philippines
and Thailand have made impressive gains, together accounting for
around 2.5% of global exports.
Looking across the AI value chain, almost all AI-enabling goods
trade is concentrated in intermediate inputs (62%) and equipment
(37%). While East Asia, ex-China, remains the dominant supplier at

every stage of the value chain, Southeast Asia has emerged as a
critical second hub. It accounts for roughly a quarter of global
exports of AI-related equipment, spanning the full value chain from
semiconductor manufacturing to the servers and other
infrastructure deployed in data centres. This is particularly
signiﬁcant because the fastest-growing areas of AI investment are
in physical infrastructure, including data centres, servers, and
semiconductor packaging and testing, where Southeast Asia has

built a strong competitive advantage.
Southeast Asia's importance is especially evident in the middle of
the value chain. Together, East Asia and Southeast Asia account for
nearly 90% of global trade in AI-related intermediate inputs, with
Southeast Asia contributing around 23%.
While growth in AI-related demand may eventually moderate,
Asia's central role in hardware and manufacturing ecosystems
should help sustain investment, trade and industrial activity. This

should keep the region a key beneﬁciary of the AI cycle for years to
come.

Is AI driving inﬂation or productivity in
Asia? And is it disrupting jobs or
transforming them?
AI's economic impact in Asia is likely to be uneven over time. Signs
of AI-related inﬂation are already visible, particularly in Korea,
where technology-related price increases are contributing
meaningfully to headline CPI. By contrast, evidence of productivity

improvements remains concentrated in parts of the technology
sector and highly AI-complementary occupations. While the
productivity beneﬁts could ultimately prove more signiﬁcant,
they’re likely to emerge gradually and take longer to be reﬂected in
economy-wide data.
AI is starting to show up in Asia's inﬂation data, especially Korea
AI is likely to be inﬂationary in the short term. This is reﬂected in the
sharp rise in memory chip prices and strong tech exports, as

demand for chips continues to outpace supply. Recent consumer
price data suggest electronics prices are beginning to recover
across parts of Asia, with the strongest increases seen in mobile
phones, computers and communication equipment.
The sharpest increases have been in Korea, where data storage
device prices rose around 40% YoY year-to-date. Inﬂation is
accelerating rapidly in these categories. Mobile phone prices rose
11% YoY in October, up from just 2% at the start of the year.

Computer-related prices accelerated to 24% YoY from 6% earlier in

the year. Mobile handset and communication device inﬂation has
also picked up in India and Indonesia, reaching 6-8% YoY.
Contribution to headline inﬂation varies across Asia, but generally
remains low given the lower weights of technology-related
components. Korea is seeing one of the region's largest increases,
with AI-related categories adding about 20bp to headline inﬂation.
In most other Asian economies, the contribution remains below 10
basis points despite price increases, reﬂecting much smaller CPI

Evidence of AI-related inﬂation in services is less clear-cut.
Information and communication services inﬂation has picked up in
Korea and Malaysia, while falling communication charges continue
to weigh on technology-related CPI components in Singapore.
Overall, however, the contribution to headline inﬂation remains
modest.
Emerging productivity gains
On productivity, the distinction between AI producers and AI users
matters. The IMF ﬁnds that advanced Asian economies like

Singapore, Korea and Japan have a relatively high concentration of
jobs where AI is more likely to augment workers than replace them,
creating greater potential for productivity gains. Around 40% of
jobs in Singapore are estimated to be highly complementary to AI.
The key question, however, is whether these gains are already
visible in the data. Direct evidence remains limited given the short
time frame of AI adoption. Australia is one of the few economies in

the region where available data draw some tentative conclusions.
Our analysis of changes in real output per worker suggests
productivity growth has improved in parts of the technology sector,
particularly telecommunications services. While it’s still too early to
attribute this entirely to AI, the evidence is consistent with
technology adoption beginning to support productivity growth in
selected industries.
Early evidence points to adaptation, not job losses

Evidence from Korea suggests AI may be reshaping labour demand
unevenly. Youth employment has weakened in highly AI-exposed
industries, while employment among workers in their 50s has
increased. India and the Philippines are among the countries most
exposed to AI because of their sizeable outsourcing sectors. Early
evidence points to job transformation and augmentation rather
than widespread displacement.
In the Philippines, employment in information and

communications, professional services, and scientiﬁc and technical
services has grown by around 4.5% over the past year. Recent
readings remain close to long-term trends. Digitally delivered
services exports continue to expand, even as the composition
shifts. Financial services and computer services are increasingly
driving growth, while the share of more traditional business process
outsourcing activities becomes less dominant.
The evidence from India points in a similar direction. Rather than

widespread layoﬀs, AI's impact appears to be showing up through
slower hiring and changing skill requirements. While AI is reducing
demand for some traditional roles, it’s creating demand for new
skills. Traditional IT ﬁrms are hiring more cautiously, but global
capability centres (GCCs) continue to expand and absorb higher-
skilled talent. As a result, India's business services exports have
more than doubled as a share of GDP since the pandemic. Even as

AI automates more routine coding and back-oﬃce tasks, demand
remains strong for higher-value activities such as software
development, analytics, AI integration and cybersecurity.

How are Asia's trade and investment
relationships shifting?
Diversiﬁcation, not deglobalisation
The key lesson from 2026 is that tariﬀs and geopolitical tensions
are reshaping trade patterns rather than materially reducing
volumes. Rather than exiting Asia, ﬁrms have responded by
diversifying production, sourcing, and investment across a wider
range of regional economies.

Tariﬀs remain an important risk, particularly if the Trump
administration refocuses on trade policy. Recent experience
suggests higher tariﬀs are more likely to shift where production
occurs than to reverse the broader diversiﬁcation trend. While
economies such as Vietnam and Thailand may face greater
scrutiny after gaining signiﬁcant US market share, tariﬀ
diﬀerentials with China are likely to remain large enough to keep
incentives for ﬁrms to maintain diversiﬁed regional supply chains.

### Page 11

Diversiﬁcation does not mean decoupling from China
Despite tariﬀs, Asia ex-China's trade surplus with the US has almost
doubled since the end of 2021, driven primarily by Taiwan, Vietnam
and Thailand. Over the same period, much of the rise in ASEAN’s
trade surplus with the US was mirrored by a corresponding decline
in China’s surplus, pointing to a reconﬁguration rather than a
reduction of regional trade ﬂows. The pattern suggests supply
chains are being reconﬁgured across Asia rather than shifting away

from the region altogether.
More importantly, production relocation has not meaningfully
reduced China's role in regional supply chains. While ﬁnal assembly
and exports to the US increasingly take place in economies such as
Vietnam and Thailand, a signiﬁcant share of intermediate inputs
still originates in China. This means supply chains are becoming
more geographically diversiﬁed but remain closely integrated with
China.
Europe is becoming a more important trade and investment

partner
Asia's trade and investment relationships are becoming more
diversiﬁed, with Europe playing an increasingly important role.
Trade ties have strengthened signiﬁcantly in recent years, and Asia
continues to gain export market share in Europe. Since 2024,
ASEAN exports to the EU have risen by around 30%. ASEAN now
exports more to Europe than Korea, Taiwan and Japan combined.
Looking ahead, a renewed push towards free-trade agreements
could provide further support. The proposed India-EU Free Trade

Agreement is particularly signiﬁcant, while a growing network of
agreements between ASEAN economies and European partners is
helping to lower barriers to trade and investment.
The investment relationship with Europe is strengthening even
faster. EU foreign direct investment into ASEAN surged to $31 billion
in 2021, overtaking the US as ASEAN's second-largest source of FDI.
More importantly, investment decisions are increasingly shaped by
strategic imperatives rather than cost competitiveness alone.

European ﬁrms increasingly view ASEAN not just as a

Has inﬂation peaked in Asia, and what
are the key risks to 2027?
Inﬂation outlook 2027: Lower headline gains but stickier core

pressures
Though it’s too early to call a peak in inﬂation in most of Asia, we
do expect prices to moderate in many parts of the region in 2027,
driven by easing energy and food prices. The largest declines
should occur in economies such as the Philippines and Singapore,
where recent inﬂation was driven primarily by fuel and food shocks.
Economies at the centre of the AI hardware ecosystem, particularly
Korea and Taiwan, may experience stickier core inﬂation as

technology-related price pressures continue to build.
While headline inﬂation should move lower, the balance of risks is
shifting from commodity-driven inﬂation towards technology-
driven inﬂation. This implies that while goods inﬂation may
moderate as energy and food pressures fade, core inﬂation could
remain stickier than expected. As a result, inﬂation may prove more

### Page 13

persistent than historical relationships with energy and food prices
alone would suggest.
Energy and food disinﬂation should drive the decline in inﬂation
Inﬂation has likely not yet peaked across most Asian economies,
but we expect it to do so in 4Q26-1Q27, with a clearer moderation
emerging from 2Q27. The key driver is energy prices.
We expect Brent crude to average around US$77/bbl in 2027,
around 14% below 2026 levels. While occasional US-Iran tensions

should keep some geopolitical risk premium in the market, we
expect a growing supply surplus to outweigh these concerns
provided there are no major disruptions to Persian Gulf exports. A
US-Iran agreement would present further downside risk to oil prices
and, by extension, inﬂation across Asia. Reﬁned fuel prices should
moderate year on year. Diesel prices could decline more sharply as
crack spreads normalise following the return of Russian supply,

although margins are likely to remain above historical averages.
Inﬂation should ease more noticeably from the second quarter of
2027, aided by favorable energy base eﬀects as oil prices were
unusually high a year earlier. Lower oil prices should also gradually
ﬁlter through to retail fuel costs, helping push headline inﬂation
lower across much of developing Asia.
The disinﬂationary impact should be most pronounced in oil-
importing economies such as the Philippines and Singapore, where

fuel has been a major driver of recent inﬂation pressures. In
Singapore, fuel prices accounted for almost 90% of the YoY increase
in headline CPI since February 2026. In Australia, transport costs
accounted for more than 100% of the increase in headline inﬂation,
oﬀset by weakness elsewhere in the CPI basket. In the Philippines,
fuel accounted for roughly 60% of the direct increase in inﬂation.
The overall impact is likely larger once second-round eﬀects

through transport, services and other fuel-sensitive categories are
taken into account. As lower oil prices feed through, these
economies should experience some of the largest declines in
inﬂation across the region.

Food should provide a second source of disinﬂation in 2027. As El
Niño fades in the second half, weather-related pressures on
agricultural production should ease. More broadly, demand and
supply conditions in key food markets appear more balanced than
in recent years, supporting a gradual moderation in food price
inﬂation.
The key wildcard is AI-related inﬂation. If these price increases
persist, second-round eﬀects could become more important
through services costs and wages in technology-intensive sectors.

Lower oil prices should help ease inﬂation,
while core pressures could persist
Source: CEIC
Are food price risks from EL Niño behind
us?
Probably not. The current El Niño is expected to remain strong to
very strong through 1Q27, and there remains a meaningful risk that
weather-related food price pressures persist into next year.
That said, a severe El Niño does not automatically translate into
higher inﬂation. Historical experience is mixed. During both the

2015-16 and 2023-24 El Niño episodes, headline inﬂation across
most of Asia was little changed, with only limited evidence of a
broad-based inﬂation shock. Taiwan was a notable exception in
2015-16. A key reason was that other commodity prices,
particularly oil and fertiliser, remained relatively subdued and were
generally declining during both episodes.
Your privacy is important

You can review and change your preferences at any time in

our Cookie policy.

### Page 15

The inﬂation impact of El Niño is likely to depend as much on
energy prices as on weather conditions themselves. A sustained
decline in oil prices would not only lower fuel inﬂation directly, but
also reduce transport, fertiliser and production costs, helping to
limit second-round eﬀects on food prices.
This matters particularly for economies such as the Philippines,
where food carries a large weight in the CPI basket. Our estimates
suggest a 20% decline in fuel prices could lower headline inﬂation

by around 100bp. Evidence from the current episode suggests a
mixed and less alarming impact across food commodities. While
rice prices have remained relatively contained in both the
Philippines and India, wheat and vegetable oil prices have risen
more noticeably. Importantly, the latter appear to reﬂect
geopolitical disruptions and broader supply constraints as much as
El Niño-related eﬀects.
Food price risks in 2027 nevertheless remain skewed to the upside.

Looking ahead, global wheat production is projected to decline by
around 3% in 2026/27, reﬂecting weaker harvests in major
producers including the US, Australia and Argentina. Rice output is
also expected to decline by 2% in 2026/27, the ﬁrst annual decline
in a decade, as El Niño weighs on harvests in major Asian
exporters. Nevertheless, energy prices are likely to remain the
dominant driver of the inﬂation outlook. Food inﬂation risks could
delay the pace of disinﬂation in some economies, but lower oil

prices should still be the primary force behind headline inﬂation
moderation across most of developing Asia in 2027.
El Nino risks persist, but the inﬂation impact
remains limited and uneven

What does all this mean for central
banks?
If we’re right about fuel prices correcting, most Asian central banks
should gain conﬁdence that inﬂation is moving sustainably back
towards target, opening the door to policy easing in the second half
of 2027 to support growth. Japan remains the key exception. Unlike
elsewhere in the region, its inﬂation is increasingly being driven by
stronger wage growth, labour shortages and ﬁrmer inﬂation

expectations. This should keep the Bank of Japan on a tightening
path for longer than the rest of the region.
Growth slowdown should strengthen the case for rate cuts in
some economies
Real policy rates are near the upper end of historical ranges across
much of Asia, with the notable exceptions of the Philippines and
Taiwan. High real rates matter because they increase borrowing
costs and tend to weigh on consumption, investment and credit
growth.

Growth is already slowing in several economies. Lagged eﬀects of
past monetary tightening could weigh on activity into 2027.
Despite still-negative real rates, the Philippines has seen growth
moderate following an aggressive tightening cycle, while Australia
is likely to face a softer growth backdrop. Higher Australian interest
rates have weighed on household demand and are beginning to
feed through to the housing market. Falling house prices there

could further constrain consumption through negative wealth
eﬀects, while weaker housing activity may weigh on investment

### Page 17

and government revenues. Australia’s labour market has also
started to show clearer signs of cooling.
Against this backdrop, Australia and the Philippines are the two
economies where we see the strongest case for rate cuts in the
second half of 2027 as policymakers shift focus from inﬂation to
supporting growth.
The case for easing is less compelling in North Asia
By contrast, growth in Korea and Taiwan is likely to moderate as the
AI-driven upswing matures and overheating concerns recede. Even

so, inﬂation should remain close to target, and economic activity
should remain relatively resilient. As a result, policy easing is less
urgent than in economies such as Australia and the Philippines.
FX considerations could delay easing in India and Indonesia
FX stability may limit the scope for rate cuts in India and Indonesia.
Unlike some regional peers, the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has
historically been reluctant to use interest rates to defend the rupee.

However, policymakers recently signalled that rates could help
support currency stability if needed. While India's growth and
inﬂation backdrop should balance better in 2027, with inﬂation
closer to target and growth moderating gradually, FX management
is an important wildcard. Given the relatively shallow hiking cycle,
we don’t expect the RBI to come under signiﬁcant pressure to
reverse course and cut rates.
Indonesia faces a diﬀerent challenge. The rupiah remains one of the

region's most interest-rate-sensitive currencies. We expect it to
remain among Asia’s weakest currencies in 2027. Even if growth
softens and inﬂation stays manageable, Bank Indonesia (BI) may
be more cautious about easing policy. Maintaining a suﬃciently
attractive rate diﬀerential and limiting FX volatility will likely remain
important policy considerations, suggesting BI may lag other
central banks in the easing cycle.
Inﬂation likely to fall close to central bank

targets by 4Q27

### Page 18

Source: CEIC, ING, National central banks
Can tech-linked currencies maintain their
leadership in 2027?
FX outlook 2027: Rotation underway, but tech currencies should
continue to lead
Asian currencies have had a mixed run in 2026. AI-linked currencies
such as the KRW, CNY, SGD and MYR generally outperformed, while
the INR, IDR and PHP weakened amid softer growth, higher energy
prices and domestic challenges. One notable exception among the
tech-linked economies is the Taiwanese dollar, which depreciated

by around 5% despite strong current account balances, as a tech-
sector sell-oﬀ in recent months triggered equity outﬂows.
In 2027, lower oil prices and a softer dollar should provide a more
supportive backdrop for Asian FX. However, we think current
account balances and capital ﬂows are likely to play a larger role
than interest-rate diﬀerentials in driving FX performance. While
some rotation into South Asian currencies is already underway, we
continue to expect tech-linked currencies, particularly the SGD in

Southeast Asia and the KRW and TWD in North Asia, to outperform.
This is thanks to strong current account surpluses and exporter FX
conversion, which provide ongoing support. By contrast, currencies
with weaker external positions and less stable capital inﬂows
remain more vulnerable.
Current account surpluses trump rate diﬀerentials in 2027
Rate diﬀerentials are becoming less supportive for EM Asia. Most
Asian central banks are nearing the end of their tightening cycles

and are likely to prepare for policy easing during 2027. Interest-rate

### Page 19

support for Asian currencies should gradually diminish, particularly
for rate-sensitive currencies such as the IDR.
Instead, current account balances and capital ﬂows are likely to
play a larger role in driving FX performance. The CNY and KRW have
been among the strongest performers since the US-Iran conﬂict in
February 2026, appreciating despite a relatively resilient DXY. This
suggests domestic fundamentals are playing a larger role in driving
Asian FX than broad dollar movements.

We remain bullish on the KRW, SGD and TWD. The AI cycle may be
plateauing, but activity remains high. Strong AI-related exports
should continue to support current account surpluses, while
exporter FX conversion should help oﬀset capital outﬂows. In Korea,
sizeable equity outﬂows since September have not prevented KRW
appreciation, highlighting the importance of exporter conversion
and capital repatriation. Measures to encourage domestic
investment and improve shareholder returns could further support

the KRW in 2027. Similarly, Japan's eﬀorts to improve domestic
returns and shareholder distributions could gradually slow capital
outﬂows over time. So, currencies backed by large current account
surpluses, particularly the KRW, TWD and CNY, should be relatively
well supported even as rate diﬀerentials become less favourable.
The main risk to this view is a sharper-than-expected slowdown in
the AI cycle, which could weaken export earnings and reduce

current account support for these currencies.
Rotation towards South Asia should continue, but selectively
Post-midterms, there’s been a rotation from North Asian to South
Asian currencies. The tech sell-oﬀ and lower oil prices have
improved valuations across parts of South Asia. Falling energy
prices should continue to support oil-importing currencies such as
the INR, IDR and PHP.
However, tighter global ﬁnancial conditions mean capital will not be

distributed evenly. We remain more constructive on the INR, as
growth should remain relatively resilient, supported by supply-
chain diversiﬁcation and continued FDI inﬂows. By contrast,
